- **agent-retried-a-failed-dbt-seed-with-t-d53042eac01252d9**: Fixes a dbt seed retry that fails because an agent changed or guessed the CSV delimiter, covering how seeds infer delimiters, inspecting the raw file, and pinning the delimiter in config. - **agent-s-fix-closed-the-db-connection-t-c8e2d382478ad758**: Fixes misdiagnosed resource leaks where the agent fixed the wrong resource. Use when a 'leak fix' does not stop the leak. - **agent-s-fix-corrupted-a-working-model-93c9189952a857da**: Teaches a dbt agent blast-radius control: run the model's own tests before and after, keep changes minimal, and review the diff so fixing a downstream test never breaks its upstream model. Isolate with per-test transactions or fresh schemas, make setup and teardown symmetric, and re-run both together in either order. Not for tests with isolated data, and not for breakage from production code changes. Not for genuinely order-dependent test logic. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-fix-passed-in-isolation-but-fa-86cc8a59fc459e29))
- **agent-s-fix-passed-locally-but-introdu-d81369761bcdb45f**: Fixes healing patches that introduce races by assuming serial execution. Use when a fix passes locally but fails on parallel CI. Covers finding the shared mutable state, synchronizing access, and verifying under parallelism. Not for races that predate the fix. - **agent-s-fix-passed-tests-locally-but-b-c37f86dd73197e43**: Teaches a dbt agent that local test passes do not guarantee a safe deploy: CI must run the full DAG in a prod-like environment, and DAG order must be verified with dbt ls before merging. Use when a locally-green fix breaks production. Not for failures that also fail locally. - **agent-s-fix-worked-on-staging-but-prod-3444acb2a9dd58e0**: Validates index choices against production data distribution, not staging's. Use when a staging-proven index does nothing in prod. - **agent-s-flake-classifier-trusts-green-3cb65a9147e8e6eb**: Fixes agents that accept a green rerun as flake evidence when the rerun ran on a bigger or different runner than the failure. - **agent-s-key-rotation-runbook-failed-ha-05129c90f39ce675**: A recovery skill for rotation runbooks that died mid-run: inventory which consumers hold which key version, restore dual-accept so nothing is broken, fix the cause, then resume the same runbook from the failed step with the same new key. Use when consumers are split across old and new keys. Triggers: 'rotation failed halfway', 'partial rotation recovery', 'some services still on old key'. Not for: double-rotation with both keys dead, leaked-key incident response, first-time rotation design. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-key-rotation-runbook-failed-ha-05129c90f39ce675))
- **agent-s-lambda-based-scheduler-hit-res-755f56058d4cda70**: Fixes a Lambda scheduler hitting reserved concurrency limits by raising the limit or serializing the stop run. Use when a Lambda-based stop scheduler throttles on reserved concurrency during the nightly run. Not for Lambda timeout errors. - **agent-s-langgraph-checkpoint-restore-f-e5092a0c724f2414**: Fixes LangGraph checkpoint restore failing on thread_id mismatch. Use when get_state or restore returns nothing for a known thread, when an agent passes the wrong thread_id, or when checkpoint namespaces changed. Not for corrupt checkpoints, for invalid updates, or for missing checkpointer config. - **agent-s-lead-scoring-zeroed-the-whole-5b23dcc1158d0ec7**: For sales-outreach (SDR) agents: Contain it, find the exact failure, fix the mechanism (not the instance), and add the guard that would have caught it. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-lead-scoring-zeroed-the-whole-5b23dcc1158d0ec7))
- **agent-s-license-check-ran-after-merge-859cf488e94d7182**: Fixes upgrade PRs that merged a dependency whose license changed to a copyleft one after the merge already happened. Use when legal flags a shipped version or the agent's license gate runs too late in the pipeline. Key trigger: the dependency's license field changed between the old and new version. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-license-check-ran-after-merge-859cf488e94d7182))
- **agent-s-license-scan-false-positived-o-12a52c9cd439815d**: Unblocks an upgrade held up by a license-scan false positive on vendored code. Use it when the scan flags a vendored file and the upgrade has been blocked for days. Key trigger: the flagged license text lives in vendor/ or a third-party directory, not in your code. Not for: a real license conflict in your own code, which needs legal review. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-license-scan-false-positived-o-12a52c9cd439815d))
- **agent-s-linkedin-login-hit-a-checkpoin-05248db3811f7d91**: Tells an SDR agent what to do when LinkedIn shows a verify-it's-you checkpoint: stop, pause the sequence, and alert a human. Never attempt to solve or bypass the challenge. Use when an automated login hits a checkpoint and the agent has no legitimate path through. Not for wrong-password loops, session refresh logic, or API access, which have their own paths. - **agent-s-linkedin-view-step-triggered-p-19dcaf076b812da6**: For sales-outreach (SDR) agents: Read the error literally: it names the exact constraint you violated. Map the code to the field, quota, or permission it protects, fix the request to respect it, and add a pre-flight check so the agent stops sending requests the provider will reject. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-linkedin-view-step-triggered-p-19dcaf076b812da6))
- **agent-s-llm-token-spend-blew-the-budge-b9155c3e38280a15**: Puts a per-run token budget and iteration ceiling on agent reasoning loops so the agent cannot spend unboundedly on itself. - **agent-s-load-generator-ran-on-the-same-cc5bf2a90899e538**: Troubleshooting guide for load-test results contaminated by running the load generator on the same machine as the service under test. Use when generator CPU saturates or results improve suspiciously after moving the generator. Shows how to detect generator-side contention, isolate generators on dedicated hosts, and add generator headroom checks to the test workflow. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-load-generator-ran-on-the-same-cc5bf2a90899e538))
- **agent-s-load-test-ddos-d-the-staging-d-733818cb3f1b1249**: Recovery and prevention guide for load tests that exhaust the database connection pool by ramping to high request rates without checking pool capacity first. Use after a staging database was overwhelmed by a load test. Shows how to size required concurrency, compare against pool limits, and re-run with a guarded ramp that watches pool waiters. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-load-test-ddos-d-the-staging-d-733818cb3f1b1249))
- **agent-s-load-test-hit-the-rate-limiter-78729f22f5ac9f74**: Troubleshooting guide for load-test results corrupted by rate limiting, where the client backs off on 429 responses and the reported p99 measures the backoff policy instead of the endpoint. Use when latency numbers look absurdly round or large. Shows how to break results down by status code, exclude throttled responses, and invalidate throttled runs. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-load-test-hit-the-rate-limiter-78729f22f5ac9f74))
- **agent-s-load-test-used-uniform-synthet-be9a937b828795dc**: Rebuilds load tests with zipfian key distributions to expose hot-key behavior. - **agent-s-local-prisma-cli-is-5-x-but-th-28c350b4f8f7db89**: Fixes Prisma migrations generated with the wrong CLI version when the agent's global CLI is newer than the project's pinned version. Not for application-code failures. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-local-run-used-cached-docker-l-f9ce3cd12342909a))
